### [110] [Symmetry analysis and exact solutions of multi-layer quasi-geostrophic problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26959)
*Serhii D. Koval,Alex Bihlo,Roman O. Popovych*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对多层准地转模型进行了扩展的对称性分析，首次完整描述了其守恒律与哈密顿结构；通过李对称方法计算了最大李不变代数与点对称伪群，并完成低余维数李约化；从中导出多个经典线性偏微分方程系统，依据垂直耦合矩阵谱性质进行积分，构造了包括Rossby波、hetons、偶极子涡旋等在内的大类精确解，并用三层海洋模型实测数据验证其物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层准地转（QG）模型是描述大气与海洋大尺度动力过程的核心非线性系统，但其对称结构、守恒律与可积性尚未被系统揭示；亟需从数学物理角度建立其对称分类与精确解构造框架，以支撑地球物理建模与观测解释。

Method: 采用李群分析方法：计算最大Lie不变代数与完整点对称伪群；对Lie代数进行一维与二维子代数分类；实施余维数为1、2、3的Lie约化；结合垂直耦合矩阵的谱分解，对约化所得线性系统（Helmholtz、Klein-Gordon等）进行显式求解；利用不变解构造技术生成精确解族。

Result: 首次确立多层QG系统的Hamiltonian结构与完整守恒律；获得任意层数情形下的最大Lie对称代数及点对称伪群；通过Lie约化导出七类经典线性PDE系统；基于耦合矩阵谱性质实现系统积分；构造含Rossby波、hetons、baroclinic eddies与局域偶极子涡旋的显式精确解族；在三层海洋模型中验证解的物理可实现性。

Conclusion: 多层准地转模型具有丰富而深层的对称结构，其Lie可约性直接通向经典线性波动与椭圆型方程；垂直耦合矩阵的谱特性是决定解行为（如传播性、局域性、稳定性）的关键数学参数；该工作为地球物理流体的解析建模与非线性现象识别提供了严格群论基础与解构造范式。

Abstract: We carry out an extended symmetry analysis of the multi-layer quasi-geostrophic problem. This model is given by a system of an arbitrary number of coupled barotropic vorticity equations. Conservation laws and a Hamiltonian structure for the general case of the model are correctly described for the first time. Using original methods, we compute the maximal Lie invariance algebra and the complete point-symmetry pseudogroup of the model. After classifying one- and two-dimensional subalgebras of the Lie invariance algebra, we exhaustively study codimension-one, -two and -three Lie reductions. Notably, among invariant submodels of the original nonlinear model, we obtain uncoupled systems of well-known linear equations, including the Helmholtz, modified Helmholtz, Laplace, Klein-Gordon, Whittaker, Bessel and linearized Benjamin-Bona-Mahony equations. Integration of these systems significantly depends on spectral properties of the model's vertical coupling matrix, which we also revisit in detail. As a result, we construct wide families of exact solutions, including rediscovered representations of stationary and travelling baroclinic Rossby waves, coherent baroclinic eddies, hetons and localized dipolar vortices. We illustrate the physical relevance of obtained solutions using real-world geophysical data for a three-layer ocean model.

</details>


### [111] [Semiclassical shape resonances for magnetic Stark Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27196)
*Kentaro Kameoka,Naoya Yoshida*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维磁Stark哈密顿量在半经典极限下的形状共振，证明了其形状共振与某参考算子离散本征值之间存在一一对应关系，从而导出共振数的Weyl律及其实部在势阱底部附近的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维磁Stark系统中由势阱和恒定磁场共同诱导的量子共振结构及其半经典渐近特性。

Method: 采用复形变技术（复平移算子定义于紧集外），将共振定义为复形变哈密顿量的复本征值；结合势阱假设与参考算子构造，建立共振与离散谱的一一对应。

Result: 证明了形状共振与参考算子离散本征值之间的一一对应；导出共振数满足Weyl律；获得共振实部在势阱底部附近的半经典渐近展开。

Conclusion: 二维磁Stark哈密顿量的形状共振具有清晰的半经典谱结构，其分布由势阱几何与磁场共同决定，且可通过参考算子的离散谱完全刻画。

Abstract: We study shape resonances of two-dimensional magnetic Stark Hamiltonians in the semiclassical limit. The magnetic field is assumed to be constant and the scalar potential is a perturbation of a linear potential. Under the assumption that the scalar potential has potential wells, the existence of a one-to-one correspondence between shape resonances of the Hamiltonian and discrete eigenvalues of a certain reference operator is proved. This implies the Weyl law for the number of resonances and the asymptotic behavior of the real parts of resonances near the bottom of a potential well. Resonances are studied as complex eigenvalues of complex distorted Hamiltonians, which is defined by the complex translation outside a compact set.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum Hall States response to toroidal geometry deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27319)
*Bruno Mera,José M. Mourão,João P. Nunes,Carolina Paiva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文运用几何量子化技术，结合复时间哈密顿演化与广义相干态变换（gCST），系统研究整数与分数量子霍尔态在环面几何形变下的响应；重点区分平直环面形变（非周期二次哈密顿量）与非平直凯勒环面形变（双周期哈密顿量诱导的Mabuchi测地线），后者在有限虚时间抵达曲率奇点，并在S¹对称性假设下获得几何与Laughlin态演化的显式解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子霍尔态对背景几何形变的鲁棒性与响应机制，尤其是从几何量子化视角统一处理平直与非平直环面形变，为拓扑序与凯勒几何之间的深层联系提供新工具。

Method: 采用复时间哈密顿演化诱导几何形变，结合广义相干态变换（gCST）追踪Laughlin态的演化；对平直形变使用平移生成元的二次非周期哈密顿量；对非平直形变则构造全局双周期哈密顿量，其虚时间流对应τ-保持的Mabuchi测地线。

Result: 对S¹不变形变，获得环面几何参数（如模参数τ）与Laughlin波函数在虚时间演化至曲率奇点全过程的显式解析表达式；确立了非平直凯勒形变中Mabuchi测地线与量子态演化的严格对应。

Conclusion: 几何量子化框架下的gCST方法为量子霍尔效应的几何响应提供了统一描述；非平直形变中的Mabuchi奇点揭示了量子态在凯勒模空间边界处的非平凡行为，暗示拓扑序与凯勒几何模空间结构存在深刻关联。

Abstract: In this paper, we apply techniques of geometric quantization to study the response of the integer and fractional quantum Hall effects to toroidal geometry deformation. The main method is that of using complex time Hamiltonian evolution to induce the geometry change and then the associated generalized coherent state transforms (gCST) to find the evolution of the Laughlin states. We consider two kinds of deformations. The first are flat toroidal deformations. Although Laughlin states for all flat toroidal geometries have been thoroughly studied before, we believe that our approach via the gCST is novel. It also serves as a testing ground to study the non-flat Kähler deformations. The Hamiltonians used in the flat deformations are quadratic in the generators of translations and therefore non periodic. The second kind of deformations involve nonflat Kähler toroidal deformations, generated by global, thus bi-periodic, Hamiltonians on the torus. The corresponding imaginary time flows are (elliptic curve modulus) $τ$-preserving Mabuchi geodesics in the space of Kähler metrics on the torus, hitting a curvature singularity in finite imaginary time. By restricting to $S^1$-invariant deformations we find explicit analytic expressions for the evolution of the toroidal geometry and of the Laughlin states all the way to the singularity.

</details>


### [113] [The Full Set of KMS-States for Abelian Kitaev Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27436)
*Danilo Polo Ojito,Emil Prodan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了阿贝尔Kitaev模型中顶点与面算符生成的子代数C是拟局域可观测量UHF代数A的一个C*-对角，并利用相应的Weyl群胚G_C将A实现为群胚C*-代数；进而通过(c_H,β)-KMS测度刻画了该模型全部KMS态，证明其在β∈[0,∞)上唯一，并确认其零温极限即为唯一的无挫基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为严格刻画阿贝尔Kitaev模型的热力学态与基态结构，需超越传统哈密顿量方法，借助C*-代数与群胚几何工具建立其动力学与平衡态的精确描述。

Method: 运用C*-代数理论，证明C⊆A为C*-对角；构造Weyl群胚G_C；将哈密顿量编码为群胚1-上循环c_H；定义并分析(c_H,β)-KMS测度以分类KMS态。

Result: 确立A≅C*(G_C)的群胚C*-代数表示；完全分类所有β-KMS态并证其唯一性；证明β→∞极限存在且等于唯一无挫基态。

Conclusion: 阿贝尔Kitaev模型的平衡统计力学由其Weyl群胚的(c_H,β)-KMS测度完全控制，且零温极限自然给出拓扑序所决定的唯一基态。

Abstract: We first prove that the subalgebra $\mathcal{C}$ generated by the vertex and face operators of an abelian Kitaev model is a $C^\ast$-diagonal of the UHF algebra $\mathcal{A}$ of quasilocal observables. This gives us access to the Weyl groupoid $\mathcal{G}_\mathcal{C}$ associated with the $C^\ast$-inclusion $\mathcal{C} \hookrightarrow \mathcal{A}$, which supplies a valuable presentation of $\mathcal{A}$ as a groupoid $C^\ast$-algebra where the dynamics of the model are generated by a groupoid 1-cocycle $c_H$. Making appeal to the notion of $(c_H,β)$-KMS measures for this groupoid, we identify the full set of KMS states of the model and prove its uniqueness for $β\in [0,\infty)$. Furthermore, we show that its limit at $β\rightarrow \infty$ exists and coincides with the unique frustration-free ground state of the model.

</details>


### [114] [Pseudo-magnetism in a strained discrete honeycomb lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27503)
*Xuenan Li,Michael I. Weinstein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非均匀应变蜂窝结构介质中诱导出的赝磁场效应，通过紧束缚模型推导出连续介质Dirac哈密顿量，并严格证明在臂椅方向平移不变的单向形变下存在沿该方向伪周期、横向指数局域的本征态；其横向局域性由一维有效Dirac算子的本征态决定，对应近似平坦的朗道能级谱；而锯齿方向平移不变的形变则不产生此类局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解蜂窝晶格（如石墨烯、光子晶体）中由缓慢变化的非均匀应变所诱导的赝磁场及其对波包动力学的影响，特别是局域化模式的存在性与结构。

Method: 从离散最近邻紧束缚模型出发，通过多尺度渐进展开和包络函数近似，导出连续极限下的有效磁Dirac哈密顿量；对保持臂椅方向平移对称性的有界梯度形变，运用谱理论与奇异摄动方法严格证明局域-扩展混合模的存在性及多尺度结构。

Result: 证明了在臂椅方向平移不变、梯度有界的非均匀应变下，存在沿臂椅方向伪周期、横向指数局域的时间谐振态；其低阶结构由一维赝磁场Dirac算子的本征态刻画；该构型对应朗道规范（Landau gauge）下的近似恒定赝磁场，产生近平坦能带（类朗道能级）；而锯齿方向对称形变无此效应。

Conclusion: 赝磁场诱导的横向局域化具有严格的几何对称性依赖：仅当应变梯度垂直于臂椅方向（即保持臂椅平移对称）时，才出现类朗道能级的高态密度局域模；该结论统一解释了石墨烯与光子蜂窝系统中的应变工程现象，并为拓扑波导设计提供理论基础。

Abstract: Slowly varying nonuniform strains of non-magnetic wave propagating media with honeycomb symmetry induce an effective- or pseudo-magnetic field, a phenomenon observed first in graphene, and later in photonic crystals and other physical settings. Starting with a discrete nearest-neighbor tight-binding model of a non-uniformly strained honeycomb medium, we derive the continuum effective magnetic Dirac Hamiltonian governing the envelope dynamics of wave packets, which are spectrally localized near a Dirac point (conical band degeneracy) of the unperturbed honeycomb. For unidirectional deformations of bounded gradient, which preserve translation invariance along the ''armchair'' direction, we prove the existence of time-harmonic states which are plane-wave like (pseudo-periodic) along the armchair direction and exponentially localized transverse to it. We also obtain the leading order multi-scale structure of such modes for small deformation gradients. Their transverse localization are determined by the eigenstates of a one dimensional effective Dirac Hamiltonian. Our rigorous results apply to deformations which induce an approximate perpendicular constant pseudo-magnetic field (Landau gauge), and yield states with nearly flat band (Landau level) spectrum and hence very high density of states. In contrast, the analogous deformation which preserves translations in the zigzag direction induces no such localization. Corroborating numerical simulations for the different deformation types are presented.

</details>


### [115] [Homothetic Hodge$-$de Rham Theory and a Geometric Regularization of Elliptic Boundary Value Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27564)
*Fereidoun Sabetghadam*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿射齐次变换的Weyl可积几何推广，通过引入以调和尺度不变形式α_d为中心的齐次规范变换，并经变量平移实现‘重线性化’，导出类Witten形变的扭曲外微积分；进而建立相应的齐次Hodge理论（含扭曲伴随算子与齐次Laplace算子），并证明紧黎曼流形上的齐次Hodge分解定理；进一步将标量齐次Laplace算子用于椭圆边值问题的弥散界面（体罚）建模，通过Weyl尺度场在超曲面附近的局域分布构造几何惩罚层，统一处理Dirichlet、Neumann或Cauchy边界条件；该框架能给出经典不相容Cauchy数据下的弱解，并构造无奇点的点源模型，保持Coulomb远场、消除原点奇异性且赋予有限场能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一处理椭圆边值问题的各类边界条件（尤其是经典不相容Cauchy数据），并构建物理上良定义的无奇点点源模型，需超越标准Riemann几何与Weyl几何的线性规范结构，引入更广义的几何自由度以编码界面效应与正则化机制。

Method: 1) 将Weyl几何中的线性规范变换推广为以调和尺度不变形式α_d为中心的仿射齐次变换；2) 通过变量平移实现‘重线性化’，导出扭曲外微分d_α与相应共轭结构；3) 定义扭曲余微分δ_α及齐次Laplace算子Δ_α = d_αδ_α + δ_αd_α；4) 在紧黎曼流形上证明齐次Hodge分解定理；5) 将Weyl尺度场设为超曲面邻域内的固定分布，解析展开低阶几何项，识别其作为体罚项的边界约束作用；6) 应用于椭圆场方程点源正则化，计算远场渐近与能量积分。

Result: 1) 建立了仿射齐次Weyl几何框架及其对应的扭曲外微积分与齐次Hodge理论；2) 证明了紧流形上齐次Hodge分解定理；3) 揭示标量齐次Laplace算子Δ_α在Weyl尺度场局域化时等价于带几何惩罚项的椭圆算子，能统一强加Dirichlet/Neumann/Cauchy条件；4) 给出经典不相容Cauchy问题的L²弱解存在性；5) 构造出具有有限自能、精确Coulomb远场、无δ-函数奇点的正则点源解。

Conclusion: 仿射齐次Weyl几何提供了一种内蕴、几何化的体罚方法，将边界约束编码于时空度规与联络的尺度结构中，从而在不引入人工界面条件的前提下，自然导出满足物理合理性的弱解；该框架不仅拓展了几何分析工具，也为高能物理中场源正则化与有效边界动力学建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce a homothetic extension of classical Weyl integrable geometry by generalizing the usual linear gauge transformations to affine homothetic transformations centered at a distinguished harmonic, scale-invariant form $α_d$. After relinearizing these affine gauge transformations via a suitable shift of variables, we obtain a twisted exterior calculus that is structurally equivalent to the Witten deformation of the de Rham complex. On this basis, we develop a corresponding homothetic Hodge theory: we define a twisted adjoint and homothetic Laplacian, and prove a homothetic Hodge decomposition theorem on compact Riemannian manifolds. In the context of partial differential equations, we show that the scalar homothetic Laplacian provides a rigorous diffuse interface (volume penalization) representation of elliptic boundary value problems. Modeling the Weyl scale field as a fixed distribution localized near a hypersurface, the resulting lower order geometric terms form a penalty layer that enforces Dirichlet, Neumann, or Cauchy data within a single geometric equation. This formulation yields consistent weak solutions even in the presence of classically incompatible Cauchy data. As an application, we construct a nonsingular model for point sources in elliptic field equations, which preserves the correct Coulombian far field while removing the core singularity and yielding finite field energy.

</details>


### [116] [Waves within a network of slowly time-modulated interfaces: time-dependent effective properties, reciprocity and high-order dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27641)
*Michaël Darche,Raphaël Assier,Sébastien Guenneau,Bruno Lombard,Marie Touboul*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在缓慢时变界面调制的一维周期性网络中的波传播问题，通过低频和二阶均匀化方法，推导出具有时变有效质量密度和杨氏模量的等效波动方程，并分析了周期性调制下k-间隙的出现以及高阶色散效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索仅通过时变界面调制即可实现体材料等效参数（如质量密度和杨氏模量）的时间依赖性调控，拓展动态超材料与非互易波控的理论基础。

Method: 采用低频均匀化（leading-order homogenisation）与二阶均匀化（second-order homogenisation）方法，结合时间依赖的弹簧-质点跳跃条件建模界面动力学，并辅以时域数值模拟验证。

Result: 1) 得到空间均匀但时间依赖的有效波动方程；2) 揭示周期性时间调制可诱导k-间隙；3) 二阶模型保持互易性但包含高阶色散项；4) 明确各模型适用范围与局限性。

Conclusion: 仅调制界面即可实现时变体有效参数，为设计动态可编程声学/弹性超材料提供了严格均匀化理论支撑；k-间隙与色散结构受时间调制对称性与尺度分离程度决定。

Abstract: We consider wave propagation through a 1D periodic network of slowly time-modulated interfaces. Each interface is modelled by time-dependent spring-mass jump conditions, where mass and rigidity interface parameters are modulated in time. Low-frequency homogenisation yields a leading-order model described by an effective time-dependent wave equation, i.e.\ a wave equation with effective mass density and Young's modulus which are homogeneous in space but depend on time. This means that time-dependent bulk effective properties can be created by an array where only interfaces are modulated in time. The occurrence of k-gaps in case of a periodic modulation is also analysed. Second-order homogenisation is then performed and leads to an effective model which is reciprocal but encapsulates higher-order dispersive effects. These findings and the limitations of the models are illustrated through time-domain simulations.

</details>


### [117] [A Concentration of Measure Phenomenon in the Principal Chiral Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27700)
*Tamer Tlas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用浓度现象研究O(N)主手性模型的大N极限，发现其配分函数等价于自由大质量理论的配分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大N极限下非线性sigma模型的渐近行为，特别是O(N)主手性模型是否能简化为可解理论。

Method: 应用测度集中现象（concentration of measure）分析路径积分在大N极限下的主导构型与涨落抑制。

Result: 证明大N极限下O(N)主手性模型的配分函数精确等价于一个自由、大质量标量场理论的配分函数。

Conclusion: O(N)主手性模型在大N极限下退耦为自由理论，表明强耦合非线性结构被测度集中效应所压制，系统有效自由度被极大简化。

Abstract: We utilize the concentration of measure phenomenon to study the large $N$ limit of the $O(N)$ principal chiral model. The partition function in this limit is demonstrated to be that of a free massive theory.

</details>


### [118] [Resonances in a Dirichlet quantum waveguide coupled to a cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27825)
*Sylwia Kondej,Nikoloz Kurtskhalia*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在二维和三维Dirichlet波导中附加腔体并引入小间隙时，嵌入本征值如何演化为共振，并定量刻画了间隙尺寸对共振极点虚部（即衰减率）的渐近依赖关系：二维情形下虚部为O(ε²)，三维矩形开口情形下为O(ε⁴)，表明特征时间尺度普适地按开口体积的平方反比标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解波导-腔体耦合系统中嵌入本征态在微扰（小间隙）下的稳定性及其向共振态的转化机制，特别是共振寿命（由极点虚部决定）对几何参数的定量依赖。

Method: 基于谱理论与散射理论，运用匹配渐近展开与复伸展方法分析拉普拉斯算子在带隙腔-波导耦合区域上的非自伴谱，精确计算共振极点在复能量平面上的位置，尤其聚焦其虚部的ε-渐近行为。

Result: 证明了二维情形下共振极点虚部主项为O(ε²)；三维矩形开口体积∝ε²时，虚部主项为O(ε⁴)；由此归纳出共振特征时间尺度τ ∼ (vol_ε)⁻²。

Conclusion: 小间隙诱导的共振衰减率具有普适的几何标度律，其虚部随开口体积呈平方反比依赖，揭示了低维与高维波导中局域-连续谱耦合强度的本质差异。

Abstract: We consider a Dirichlet waveguide in $\mathbb{R}^n$ ($n = 2,3$) with an attached cavity. We show that if the cavity admits a small gap, then the original embedded eigenvalues turn into resonances. The main question we address is how the size of the gap affects the resonant properties, in particular the imaginary part of the resonant pole. For example, in the case of a two dimensional waveguide with a gap of size $\varepsilon$, we show that the leading order term of the resonance behaves as $\mathcal O (\varepsilon^2)$. In the three-dimensional case, if the aperture is defined by a rectangular opening with volume proportional to $\varepsilon^2$, the resonant component behaves as $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^4)$. This shows that, in the analyzed class of models, the characteristic time scale associated with the resonances is generically of order $\mathcal{O}((\mathrm{vol}_\varepsilon)^{-2})$, where $\mathrm{vol}_\varepsilon$ denotes the volume of the aperture inducing the resonance.

</details>


### [119] [A symmetry formula for correlation functions in the superintegrable chiral Potts spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27930)
*Haoran Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了周期性N态超可积手性Potts自旋链中两点关联函数的一个精确有限体积对称性公式，并验证了Fabricius与McCoy提出的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Fabricius和McCoy关于N态超可积手性Potts模型中关联函数对称性的猜想，拓展此前仅针对三态情形的结论至任意N及所有平移本征子空间。

Method: 利用模型的超可积性、周期边界条件及哈密顿量与单格点平移算符的共同本征态结构，推导并严格证明两点函数满足反射对称关系⟨Z₀ʳ Z_R^†ʳ⟩* = ⟨Z₀ʳ Z_{L−R}^†ʳ⟩。

Result: 对任意链长L、任意N ≥ 2及每个哈密顿量与平移算符的共同本征态，证明了该复共轭反射对称性；特别地，当L为偶数时，中点关联⟨Z₀ʳ Z_{L/2}^†ʳ⟩为实数。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了超可积手性Potts链在有限体积下严格的关联对称性，完成了对Fabricius–McCoy猜想的严格证明，并将三态情形推广至一般N态及全平移本征子空间。

Abstract: We prove an exact finite-volume symmetry formula for two-point functions in the periodic $N$-state superintegrable chiral Potts spin chain. We show that, for every chain length $L$ and every simultaneous eigenvector of the Hamiltonian and the one-site translation operator, the correlations satisfy $\langle Z_0^r Z_R^{\dagger r}\rangle^*=\langle Z_0^r Z_{L-R}^{\dagger r}\rangle$ for $1\leqslant r\leqslant N-1$. Hence, whenever $L$ is even, the midpoint correlation $\langle Z_0^r Z_{L/2}^{\dagger r}\rangle$ is real. Then we generalise the three-state chain case to arbitrary $N$ and to every translation eigensector. This resolves a conjecture of Fabricius and McCoy.

</details>


### [120] [A Damage-Driven Model for Duchenne Muscular Dystrophy: Early-Stage Dynamics and Invasion Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28147)
*Gaetana Gambino,Francesco Gargano,Alessandra Rizzo,Vincenzo Sciacca*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于损伤驱动范式的杜氏肌营养不良（DMD）空间扩展数学模型，描述健康组织、受损肌纤维、免疫细胞与炎症信号之间的反应-扩散-趋化相互作用；证明了系统的全局适定性；线性分析表明不存在Turing不稳定性，疾病进展由损伤介导的入侵过程主导；推导出损伤再生阈值与病理锋面的最小传播速度，并证实其为pulled-front机制；数值模拟验证了衰减与入侵的相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立能刻画DMD早期空间病程演化的定量框架，解释临床观察到的局灶性损伤扩展现象，而非传统均匀退化假设。

Method: 构建四组分反应-扩散-趋化偏微分方程系统；运用能量估计与不变区域方法证明全局经典解的存在唯一性；对健康稳态进行线性稳定性分析与色散关系研究；采用Fisher-KPP型前沿理论推导入侵阈值与最小波速；结合数值模拟（有限差分/谱方法）验证解析结论。

Result: 1) 系统全局适定；2) 扩散不诱发Turing失稳，空间异质性非自组织产生；3) 疾病以损伤驱动的入侵波形式传播；4) 存在临界损伤再生数R_d > 1为入侵判据；5) 病理锋面为pulled front，最小传播速度c* = 2√(Dχα)，其中D为免疫细胞扩散系数，χ为趋化敏感度，α为损伤生成率；6) 数值重现衰减（R_d < 1）与入侵（R_d > 1）的相变。

Conclusion: DMD早期空间进展本质是损伤-免疫正反馈引发的单向入侵过程，其动力学由pulled-front机制支配；该模型排除了扩散驱动斑图形成的可能，强调局灶损伤扩展是空间模式的起源，为靶向损伤起始点的干预策略提供理论依据。

Abstract: We introduce a spatially extended mathematical model for Duchenne muscular dystrophy based on a damage-driven paradigm, in which immune recruitment is triggered by tissue injury. The model is formulated as a reaction--diffusion--chemotaxis system describing the interaction between healthy tissue, damaged fibers, immune cells and inflammatory signals. We establish the global well-posedness of the system and investigate the early-stage dynamics through linearization around the healthy equilibrium. Our analysis shows that diffusion does not induce Turing instabilities, so that spatial heterogeneity cannot arise from diffusion-driven mechanisms. Instead, disease progression occurs through invasion processes. We derive explicit conditions for the onset of invasion, interpreted as an effective damage reproduction threshold and characterize the minimal propagation speed of pathological fronts, showing that the dynamics is governed by a pulled-front mechanism. Numerical simulations support the analytical results and confirm the transition between decay and invasion. These results provide a mathematical framework for early-stage disease progression and indicate that spatial patterns arise from the expansion of localized damage rather than from intrinsic pattern-forming mechanisms.

</details>


### [121] [Quasi-local probability averaging in the context of cutoff regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28235)
*A. V. Ivanov,I. V. Korenev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意维欧氏空间中拉普拉斯算子的平均基本解的性质，引入了适用于概率平均的核类，并给出了变形基本解及其在零点取值的新表示；此外，结合具体量子场论模型探讨了重整化性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拉普拉斯算子平均基本解的结构性质，旨在为量子场论中的重整化问题提供新的数学工具和解析表示。

Method: 采用调和分析与概率测度相结合的方法，构造适合概率平均的核类，推导变形基本解的积分表示及零点渐近行为。

Result: 获得了变形基本解及其在原点取值的显式新表示；建立了与特定量子场模型（如ϕ⁴理论）重整化群流相关的联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维欧氏空间中拉普拉斯算子的基本解提供了概率加权平均框架，拓展了其在量子场论重整化分析中的适用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the properties of averaged fundamental solutions of a special type for Laplace operators in the Euclidean space of an arbitrary dimension. We consider a class of kernels suitable for probabilistic averaging, and propose new representations for the deformed fundamental solutions and their values at zero. In addition, we give examples related to specific quantum field models in the context of studying renormalization properties.

</details>


### [122] [Three non-Hermitian random matrix universality classes of complex edge statistics: Spacing ratios and distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28457)
*Gernot Akemann,Georg Angermann,Noah Aygün,Adam Mielke,Patricia Päßler,Christoph Raitzig,Tobias Winkler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非厄米随机矩阵三大对称类（A、AI†、AII†）的局域体态与边缘统计特性，重点分析复间距比（complex spacing ratio）与最近邻间距分布（NN spacing distribution），解析推导了Ginibre系综有限N下的复间距比，并提出椭圆Ginibre系综N=3参数化surmise；数值上对比三类系综与2D泊松过程在体态/边缘的统计行为，指出复间距比在边缘处未能完全展平局域统计，而NN间距小距离行为均呈现普适立方排斥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展非厄米随机矩阵局域统计普适性：从已知的三大体态普适类延伸至对应的边缘普适类，并系统检验复间距比与NN间距分布作为局域统计探针的有效性与局限性，尤其关注边缘区域是否仍满足普适立方排斥及复间距比的展平能力。

Method: 解析方法：基于条件点过程简化复间距比表达式，严格处理Ginibre系综有限N情形，并构造椭圆Ginibre系综N=3参数化surmise；数值方法：蒙特卡洛模拟三类高斯系综（Ginibre、复对称、复自对偶）的特征值谱，计算复间距比分布、其矩、NN间距分布，并与2D泊松过程对比；物理建模：采用二维库仑气体类比，将不同对称类边缘统计映射至不同逆温度β的等效相互作用系统。

Result: 1) Ginibre系综复间距比在大N极限下原近似收敛性获严格解释；2) 椭圆Ginibre系综N=3 surmise成功插值至GUE极限；3) 三类边缘统计表现出由β调控的差异化排斥强度；4) 复间距比在边缘处无法完全展平，暗示其对边缘局域统计敏感度不足；5) 所有三类在体态与边缘的小间距极限下NN分布均符合r³普适标度律。

Conclusion: 非厄米三大对称类的局域边缘统计具有普适性，但复间距比作为探测工具在边缘失效，需辅以NN间距分布；立方排斥r³是体态与边缘共有的普适低能标行为；边缘统计可统一理解为β-可调的2D Coulomb气体，证实了非厄米普适类的热力学类比框架。

Abstract: The conjectured three generic local bulk statistics amongst all non-Hermitian random matrix symmetry classes have recently been extended to three generic local edge statistics. We study analytically and numerically complex spacing ratios and nearest-neighbour (NN) spacing distributions that characterise such local statistics. We choose the three simplest representatives of these universality classes, given by the Gaussian ensembles of complex Ginibre, complex symmetric and complex self-dual matrices, denoted by class A, AI$^†$ and AII$^†$. In the first part, we analytically study the complex spacing ratio in class A, at finite matrix size $N$. Introducing a conditional point process, we simplify existing expressions and show why an uncontrolled approximation introduced earlier converges well in the large-$N$ limit in the bulk. When specifying to the elliptic Ginibre ensemble, we present a parameter-dependent $N=3$ surmise for the complex spacing ratio, interpolating to that of the Gaussian unitary ensemble (GUE), where such a surmise is very accurate. In the second numerical part, we compare complex spacing ratios, its moments, and NN spacing distributions for all three ensembles with that of uncorrelated points, the two-dimensional (2D) Poisson process, both in the bulk and at the edge. The varying degree of repulsion within these different edge universality classes can be well understood in terms of an effective 2D Coulomb gas description, at different values of inverse temperature $β$. We find indications that the complex spacing ratio does not fully unfold the local statistics at the edge. Finally we verify that for small argument, in all three symmetry classes the NN spacing distributions in the bulk and at the edge are consistent with the universal cubic repulsion.

</details>


### [123] [Geometry of the Ising persistence problem and the universal Bonnet-Manin Painlevé VI distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28632)
*Ivan Dornic,Robert Conte*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非马尔可夫随机过程的全持久概率分布，发现其由一个特殊的Painlevé VI方程控制，该方程源于与可积sech核相关的Fredholm Pfaffian结构；持久指数被重新解释为该Painlevé VI解在无穷远处的渐近值，并具有明确的微分几何意义——即对应一族Bonnet曲面的平均曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源自自旋系统及关联体系中的首达时问题，特别是非马尔可夫持续性（persistence）行为的精确刻画需求。

Method: 利用可积sech核K_sech=1/(2πcosh[(x−y)/2])的Fredholm Pfaffian结构，导出持久概率的精确Pfaffian分解；通过分析其偶/奇Fredholm行列式，建立对应的二阶非线性常微分方程，并识别为Painlevé VI型；进一步建立该Painlevé解与ℝ³中Bonnet曲面平均曲率的几何对应。

Result: 持久概率分布完全由特定Painlevé VI方程（Manin系数[0,0,0,0]）控制；Derrida–Hakim–Pasquier普适持久指数成为该Painlevé解在无穷远的渐近极限；该解等同于一族Bonnet曲面的平均曲率，从而赋予持久指数以微分几何诠释。

Conclusion: 非马尔可夫持久性问题可通过可积核的Pfaffian结构严格求解，并自然地嵌入Painlevé transcendent理论与曲面几何框架之中，揭示了临界动力学、可积系统与微分几何之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We determine the full persistence probability distribution for a non-Markovian stochastic process, motivated by first-passage questions arising in interacting spin systems and allied systems. We show that this distribution is governed by a distinguished Painlevé VI system arising from an exact Fredholm Pfaffian structure associated with the integrable sech kernel, $K_{\mathrm{sech}}=1/(2 π\cosh[(x-y)/2])$. The universal persistence exponent originally obtained by Derrida, Hakim and Pasquier is recovered as an asymptotic observable and acquires a natural geometric interpretation. In the stationary scaling regime, the persistence probability admits an exact Pfaffian decomposition into even and odd Fredholm determinants of the integrable \emph{sech} kernel. These determinants are controlled by a unique global solution of a second-order nonlinear ordinary differential equation, which is identified as a particular Painlevé VI equation. The corresponding Painlevé VI connection problem determines the persistence exponent as a limiting value at infinity.
  We further show that the Painlevé VI system governing persistence admits a direct geometric interpretation: the relevant solution coincides with the mean curvature of a one-parameter family of Bonnet surfaces immersed in $\mathbb R^3$. A folding transformation between such surfaces singles out the Painlevé VI equation with Manin coefficients $[0,0,0,0]$, which in particular governs the universal persistence distribution in the symmetric Ising case. In this framework, the persistence exponent is identified with the asymptotic mean curvature of the associated surface.

</details>


### [124] [Invariant measures of randomized quantum trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28664)
*Tristan Benoist,Sascha Lill,Cornelia Vogel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在探针可观测量随机化条件下量子轨迹的性质，证明了非奇异随机化能确保量子轨迹纯化并存在唯一不变概率测度，并引入了‘乘性本原性’这一新的量子信道遍历性概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机化探针可观测量对量子轨迹长期行为的影响，特别是其纯化性质与不变测度的存在性与正则性。

Method: 运用量子马尔可夫链、量子信道理论、遍历性分析及算子半群方法，定义并研究乘性本原性（multiplicative primitivity）这一新遍历性条件。

Result: 证明非奇异随机化下量子轨迹必然纯化，存在唯一不变概率测度；该测度具有Hölder连续性；乘性本原性是介于本原性（primitivity）与正性提升性（positivity improving）之间的严格中间条件；给出若干典型量子信道（如去相位、振幅阻尼）的不变测度显式计算。

Conclusion: 探针可观测量的随机化是一种有效的正则化机制，能恢复量子轨迹的纯化与遍历性；乘性本原性为刻画量子信道强混合行为提供了更精细的判据。

Abstract: Quantum trajectories are Markov chains modeling quantum systems subjected to repeated indirect measurements. Their stationary regime depends on what observables are measured on the probes used to indirectly measure the system. In this article we explore the properties of quantum trajectories when the choice of probe observable is randomized. The randomization induces some regularization of the quantum trajectories. We show that non-singular randomization ensures that quantum trajectories purify and therefore accept a unique invariant probability measure. We furthermore study the regularity of that invariant measure. In that endeavour, we introduce a new notion of ergodicity for quantum channels, which we call multiplicative primitivity. It is a priory stronger than primitivity but weaker than positivity improving. Finally, we compute some invariant measures for canonical quantum channels and explore the limits of our assumptions with several examples.

</details>
